recipients and records them for the feed.\n10\t */\n11\tcontract Payouts {\n12\t    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n13\t\n14\t    event Paid(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 amount, recipients and records them for the feed.\n */\ncontract Payouts {\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    event Paid(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 amount, string memo);\n\n    constructor(address _usdc) {\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    }\n\n    function pay(address to, uint256 amount, string calldata memo) external {\n        require(to != address(0), \"Payouts: zero (optional)\"\n94\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n95\t              value={memo}\n96\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n97\t            />\n98\t\n99\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n100\t              <button\n101\t                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n102\t                disabled={isPending}\n103\t                onClick={handleApprove}\n104\t              >\n105\t                Approve\n106\t              </button>\n107\t              <button\n108\t                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n109\t                disabled={isPending}\n110\t                onClick={handlePay}\n111\t              >\n112\t                Send Payout\n113\t              </button>\n114\t            </div>\n115\t          </>\n116\t        )}\n117\t\n118\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n119\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n120\t          {(payouts ?? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":138,"startLine":1,"totalLines":138}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Nq9Bxg4X953nnwpWi2dYWU","type":"tool_result","content":"  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  eventName,\n  fromBlock,\n  toBlock,\n  chainId,\n  filters,\n  blockData,\n  transactionData,\n  receiptData,\n  watch,\n  enabled = true,\n  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n      console.log(\n        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. It can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s)\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [selectedNetwork.id]);\n\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient({\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n  });\n  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [lastFetchedBlock, setLastFetchedBlock] = useState<bigint | null>(null);\n  const [isPollingActive, setIsPollingActive] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({ watch: watch, chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n\n  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const event =\n    deployedContractData &&\n    ((deployedContractData.abi as Abi).find(part => part.type === \"event\" && part.name === eventName) as AbiEvent);\n\n  const isContractAddressAndClientReady = Boolean(deployedContractData?.address) && Boolean(publicClient);\n\n  const fromBlockValue =\n    fromBlock !== undefined\n      ? fromBlock\n      : BigInt(\n          deployedContractData && \"deployedOnBlock\" in deployedContractData\n            ? deployedContractData.deployedOnBlock || 0\n            : 0,\n        );\n\n  const query = useInfiniteQuery({\n    queryKey: [\n      \"eventHistory\",\n      {\n        contractName,\n        address: deployedContractData?.address,\n        eventName,\n        fromBlock: fromBlockValue?.toString(),\n        toBlock: toBlock?.toString(),\n        chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n        filters: JSON.stringify(filters, replacer),\n        blocksBatchSize: blocksBatchSize.toString(),\n      },\n    ],\n    queryFn: async ({ pageParam }) => {\n      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady) return undefined;\n\n      // Calculate the toBlock for this batch\n      let batchToBlock = toBlock;\n      const batchEndBlock = pageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize) - 1n;\n      const maxBlock = toBlock || (blockNumber ? BigInt(blockNumber) : undefined);\n      if (maxBlock) {\n        batchToBlock = batchEndBlock < maxBlock ? batchEndBlock : maxBlock;\n      }\n\n      const data = await getEvents(\n        {\n          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n          event,\n          fromBlock: pageParam,\n          toBlock: batchToBlock,\n          args: filters,\n        },\n        publicClient,\n        { blockData, transactionData, receiptData },\n      );\n\n      setLastFetchedBlock(batchToBlock || blockNumber || 0n);\n\n      return data;\n    },\n    enabled: enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && !isPollingActive, // Disable when polling starts\n    initialPageParam: fromBlockValue,\n    getNextPageParam: (lastPage, allPages, lastPageParam) => {\n      if (!blockNumber || fromBlockValue >= blockNumber) return undefined;\n\n      const nextBlock = lastPageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize);\n\n      // Don't go beyond the specified toBlock or current block\n      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n\n      if (nextBlock > maxBlock) return undefined;\n\n      return nextBlock;\n    },\n    select: data => {\n      const events = data.pages.flat() as unknown as UseScaffoldEventHistoryData<\n        TContractName,\n        TEventName,\n        TBlockData,\n        TTransactionData,\n        TReceiptData\n      >;","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"d6c37a74-3217-4123-aae9-478931dc1156","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:42:27.312Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n>({\n  contractName,\n  eventName,\n  fromBlock,\n  toBlock,\n  chainId,\n  filters,\n  blockData,\n  transactionData,\n  receiptData,\n  watch,\n  enabled = true,\n  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n      console.log(\n        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154111Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/.claude/skills/qa\n\n# dApp QA — Pre-Ship Audit For Scaffold-ETH 2 Builds\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"The app deployed, so we are done.\"** For SE2 builds, shipping includes UX correctness, metadata, RPC reliability, contract verification, and branding cleanup.\n\n**\"The flow is obvious.\"** If Connect, Network, Approve, and Action are not strictly one-at-a-time with proper pending states, users will make duplicate or failing transactions.\n\n**\"SE2 defaults are fine in production.\"** Default README/footer/title/favicon and default RPC fallbacks are template scaffolding, not production decisions.\n\n**\"Pass means no console errors.\"** QA pass/fail here is behavioral and user-facing: real wallet flow, mobile deep-link behavior, readable errors, and trust signals must be validated.\n\nGive this to a fresh agent after the dApp is built. The reviewer should:\n\n1. Read the source code (`app/`, `components/`, `contracts/`)\n2. Open the app in a browser and click through every flow\n3. Check every item below — report PASS/FAIL, don't fix\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: Wallet Flow — Button Not Text\n\nOpen the app with NO wallet connected.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Text saying \"Connect your wallet to play\" / \"Please connect to continue\" / any paragraph telling the user to connect\n- ✅ **PASS:** A big, obvious Connect Wallet **button** is the primary UI element\n\n**This is the most common AI agent mistake.** Every stock LLM writes a `<p>Please connect your wallet</p>` instead of rendering `<RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />`.\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: Four-State Button Flow\n\nThe app must show exactly ONE primary button at a time, progressing through:\n\n```\n1. Not connected  → Connect Wallet button\n2. Wrong network  → Switch to [Chain] button\n3. Needs approval → Approve button\n4. Ready          → Action button (Stake/Deposit/Swap)\n```\n\nCheck specifically:\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Approve and Action buttons both visible simultaneously\n- ❌ **FAIL:** No network check — app tries to work on wrong chain and fails silently\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Main onchain CTA renders instead of a \"Switch to [Chain]\" button when the connected wallet is on the wrong network. SE-2's header `WrongNetworkDropdown` is **not sufficient** — the action button itself must become the switch CTA, or the user clicks Sign/Stake/Deposit on the wrong chain and eats a silent wagmi error.\n- ❌ **FAIL:** User can click Approve, sign in wallet, come back, and click Approve again while tx is pending\n- ✅ **PASS:** One button at a time. Approve button shows spinner, stays disabled until block confirms onchain. Then switches to the action button.\n- ✅ **PASS:** Action button's render path branches on `useChainId() === targetNetwork.id` (or equivalent); mismatch renders a `useSwitchChain`-driven \"Switch to [Chain]\" button in the **same slot** as the primary CTA.\n\n**In the code:** the button's `disabled` prop must be tied to `isPending` from `useScaffoldWriteContract`. Verify it uses `useScaffoldWriteContract` (waits for block confirmation), NOT raw wagmi `useWriteContract` (resolves on wallet signature):\n\n```\ngrep -rn \"useWriteContract\" packages/nextjs/\n```\nAny match outside scaffold-eth internals → bug.\n\n**Watch out: two gaps, both allow double-approve.**\n\n`isPending` from wagmi drops to `false` when the wallet returns the tx hash — not when the tx confirms. `writeContractAsync` is still awaiting confirmation. During that window `isPending = false` AND `approveCooldown = false` → button re-enables mid-flight.\n\nFix requires TWO states:\n- `approvalSubmitting` — set at top of handler, cleared in `finally {}` (covers click→hash gap)\n- `approveCooldown` — set after `await` resolves, cleared after 4s + refetch (covers confirm→cache gap)\n\n```tsx\nconst [approvalSubmitting, setApprovalSubmitting] = useState(false);\nconst [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n\nconst handleApprove = async () => {\n  if (approvalSubmitting || approveCooldown) return;\n  setApprovalSubmitting(true);\n  try {\n    await approveWrite({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [spender, amount] });\n    setApproveCooldown(true);\n    setTimeout(() => { setApproveCooldown(false); refetchAllowance(); }, 4000);\n  } catch (e) {\n    notifyError(\"Approval failed\");\n  } finally {\n    setApprovalSubmitting(false); // must be finally — releases on rejection too\n  }\n};\n\n<button disabled={isPending || approvalSubmitting || approveCooldown}>\n```\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Button `disabled` only reads `isPending` or only `approveCooldown`\n- ❌ **FAIL:** No `approvalSubmitting` state, or it's not cleared in `finally {}`\n- ✅ **PASS:** `disabled={isPending || approvalSubmitting || approveCooldown}` with both states managed correctly\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: SE2 Branding Removal\n\nAI agents treat the scaffold as sacred and leave all default branding in place.\n\n- [ ] **Footer:** Remove BuidlGuidl links, \"Built with 🏗️ SE2\", \"Fork me\" link, support links. Replace with project's own repo link or clean it out\n- [ ] **Tab title:** Must be the app name, NOT \"Scaffold-ETH 2\" or \"SE-2 App\" or \"App Name | Scaffold-ETH 2\"\n- [ ] **README:** Must describe THIS project. Not the SE2 template README. Remove \"Built with Scaffold-ETH 2\" sections and SE2 doc links\n- [ ] **Favicon:** Must not be the SE2 default\n\n---\n\n## Important: Contract Address Display\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** The deployed contract address appears nowhere on the page\n- ✅ **PASS:** Contract address displayed using `<Address/>` component (blockie, ENS, copy, explorer link)\n\nAgents display the connected wallet address but forget to show the contract the user is interacting with.\n\n---\n\n## Important: Address Input — Always `<AddressInput/>`\n\n**EVERY input that accepts an Ethereum address must use `<AddressInput/>`, not a plain `<input type=\"text\">`.**\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `<input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"0x...\" value={addr} onChange={e => setAddr(e.target.value)} />`\n- ✅ **PASS:** `<AddressInput value={addr} onChange={setAddr} placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" />`\n\n`<AddressInput/>` gives you ENS resolution (type \"vitalik.eth\" → resolves to address), blockie avatar preview, validation, and paste handling. A raw text input is unacceptable for address collection.\n\n**In SE2, it's in `@scaffold-ui/components`:**\n```typescript\nimport { AddressInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\n// or\nimport { AddressInput } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\"; // if re-exported\n```\n\n**Quick check:**\n```bash\ngrep -rn 'type=\"text\"' packages/nextjs/app/ | grep -i \"addr\\|owner\\|recip\\|0x\"\ngrep -rn 'placeholder=\"0x' packages/nextjs/app/\n```\nAny match → **FAIL**. Replace with `<AddressInput/>`.\n\nThe pair: `<Address/>` for **display**, `<AddressInput/>` for **input**. Always.\n\n---\n\n## Important: USD Values\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Token amounts shown as \"1,000 TOKEN\" or \"0.5 ETH\" with no dollar value\n- ✅ **PASS:** \"0.5 ETH (~$1,250)\" with USD conversion\n\nAgents never add USD values unprompted. Check every place a token or ETH amount is displayed, including inputs.\n\n---\n\n## Important: OG Image Must Be Absolute URL\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `images: [\"/thumbnail.jpg\"]` — relative path, breaks unfurling everywhere\n- ✅ **PASS:** `images: [\"https://yourdomain.com/thumbnail.jpg\"]` — absolute production URL\n\nQuick check:\n```\ngrep -n \"og:image\\|images:\" packages/nextjs/app/layout.tsx\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important: RPC & Polling Config\n\nOpen `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`:\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `pollingInterval: 30000` (default — makes the UI feel broken, 30 second update lag)\n- ✅ **PASS:** `pollingInterval: 3000`\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Using default Alchemy API key that ships with SE2\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Code references `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_*` but the variable isn't actually set in the deployment environment (Vercel/hosting). Falls back to public RPC like `mainnet.base.org` which is rate-limited\n- ✅ **PASS:** `rpcOverrides` uses `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_*` variables AND the env var is confirmed set on the hosting platform\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx` still includes bare `http()` fallback transport (silently hits public RPCs in parallel, causing rate limits)\n- ✅ **PASS:** Bare `http()` fallback removed; only intended configured transports remain\n\n**Verify the env var is set, not just referenced.** AI agents will change the code to use `process.env`, see the pattern matches PASS, and move on — without ever setting the actual variable on Vercel/hosting. Check:\n```bash\nvercel env ls | grep RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important: SE2 `externalContracts.ts` Registration\n\nScaffold hooks only work with contracts registered in `deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated) or `externalContracts.ts` (manual). If external contracts are not registered, frontend reads/writes silently fail.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Frontend code references token/protocol contracts that are missing from `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `deployedContracts.ts` manually edited to add external contracts\n- ✅ **PASS:** All external contracts are defined in `externalContracts.ts` with correct chain, address, and ABI\n\nExample:\n```typescript\nexport default {\n  8453: {\n    USDC: {\n      address: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\",\n      abi: [...],\n    },\n  },\n} as const;\n```\n\nNever edit `deployedContracts.ts` directly. It is regenerated on deploy.\n\n---\n\n## Important: Dark Mode — No Hardcoded Dark Backgrounds\n\nAI agents love the aesthetic of a dark UI and will hardcode it directly on the page wrapper:\n\n```tsx\n// ❌ FAIL — hardcoded black background, ignores system preference AND DaisyUI theme\n<div className=\"min-h-screen bg-[#0a0a0a] text-white\">\n```\n\nThis bypasses the entire DaisyUI theme system. Light-mode users get a black page. The `SwitchTheme` toggle in the SE2 header stops working. `prefers-color-scheme` is ignored.\n\n**Check for this pattern:**\n```bash\ngrep -rn 'bg-\\[#0\\|bg-black\\|bg-gray-9\\|bg-zinc-9\\|bg-neutral-9\\|bg-slate-9' packages/nextjs/app/\n```\nAny match on a root layout div or page wrapper → **FAIL**.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Root page wrapper uses a hardcoded hex color or Tailwind dark bg class (`bg-[#0a0a0a]`, `bg-black`, `bg-zinc-900`, etc.)\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `SwitchTheme` toggle is present in the header but the page ignores `data-theme` entirely\n- ✅ **PASS:** All backgrounds use DaisyUI semantic variables — `bg-base-100`, `bg-base-200`, `text-base-content`\n- ✅ **PASS (dark-only exception):** Theme is explicitly forced via `data-theme=\"dark\"` on `<html>` **AND** the `<SwitchTheme/>` component is removed from the header\n\n**The fix:**\n```tsx\n// ✅ CORRECT — responds to light/dark toggle and prefers-color-scheme\n<div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important: Phantom Wallet in RainbowKit\n\nPhantom is NOT in the SE2 default wallet list. A lot of users have Phantom — if it's missing, they can't connect.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Phantom wallet not in the RainbowKit wallet list\n- ✅ **PASS:** `phantomWallet` is in `wagmiConnectors.tsx`\n\n---\n\n## Important: Mobile Deep Linking\n\n**RainbowKit v2 / WalletConnect v2 does NOT auto-deep-link to the wallet app.** It relies on push notifications instead, which are slow and unreliable. You must implement deep linking yourself.\n\nOn mobile, when a user taps a button that needs a signature, it must open their wallet app. Test this: open the app on a phone, connect a wallet via WalletConnect, tap an action button — does the wallet app open with the transaction ready to sign?\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Nothing happens, user has to manually switch to their wallet app\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Deep link fires BEFORE the transaction — user arrives at wallet with nothing to sign\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `window.location.href = \"rainbow://\"` called before `writeContractAsync()` — navigates away and the TX never fires\n- ❌ **FAIL:** It opens the wrong wallet (e.g. opens MetaMask when user connected with Rainbow)\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Deep links inside a wallet's in-app browser (unnecessary — you're already in the wallet)\n- ✅ **PASS:** Every transaction button fires the TX first, then deep links to the correct wallet app after a delay\n\n### How to implement it\n\n**Pattern: `writeAndOpen` helper.** Fire the write call first (sends the TX request over WalletConnect), then deep link after a delay to switch the user to their wallet:\n\n```typescript\nconst writeAndOpen = useCallback(\n  <T,>(writeFn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {\n    const promise = writeFn(); // Fire TX — does gas estimation + WC relay\n    setTimeout(openWallet, 2000); // Switch to wallet AFTER request is relayed\n    return promise;\n  },\n  [openWallet],\n);\n\n// Usage — wraps every write call:\nawait writeAndOpen(() => gameWrite({ functionName: \"click\", args: [...] }));\n```\n\n**Why 2 seconds?** `writeContractAsync` must estimate gas, encode calldata, and relay the signing request through WalletConnect's servers. 300ms is too fast — the wallet won't have received the request yet.\n\n**Detecting the wallet:** `connector.id` from wagmi says `\"walletConnect\"`, NOT `\"rainbow\"` or `\"metamask\"`. You must check multiple sources:\n\n```typescript\nconst openWallet = useCallback(() => {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return;\n  const isMobile = /iPhone|iPad|iPod|Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);\n  if (!isMobile || window.ethereum) return; // Skip if desktop or in-app browser\n\n  // Check connector, wagmi storage, AND WalletConnect session data\n  const allIds = [connector?.id, connector?.name,\n    localStorage.getItem(\"wagmi.recentConnectorId\")]\n    .filter(Boolean).join(\" \").toLowerCase();\n\n  let wcWallet = \"\";\n  try {\n    const wcKey = Object.keys(localStorage).find(k => k.startsWith(\"wc@2:client\"));\n    if (wcKey) wcWallet = (localStorage.getItem(wcKey) || \"\").toLowerCase();\n  } catch {}\n  const search = `${allIds} ${wcWallet}`;\n\n  const schemes: [string[], string][] = [\n    [[\"rainbow\"], \"rainbow://\"],\n    [[\"metamask\"], \"metamask://\"],\n    [[\"coinbase\", \"cbwallet\"], \"cbwallet://\"],\n    [[\"trust\"], \"trust://\"],\n    [[\"phantom\"], \"phantom://\"],\n  ];\n\n  for (const [keywords, scheme] of schemes) {\n    if (keywords.some(k => search.includes(k))) {\n      window.location.href = scheme;\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n}, [connector]);\n```\n\n**Key rules:**\n1. **Fire TX first, deep link second.** Never `window.location.href` before the write call\n2. **Skip deep link if `window.ethereum` exists** — means you're already in the wallet's in-app browser\n3. **Check WalletConnect session data** in localStorage — `connector.id` alone won't tell you which wallet\n4. **Use simple scheme URLs** like `rainbow://` — not `rainbow://dapp/...` which reloads the page\n5. **Wrap EVERY write call** — approve, action, claim, batch — not just the main one\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: Contract Verification on Block Explorer\n\nAfter deploying, every contract MUST be verified on the block explorer. Unverified contracts are a trust red flag — users can't read the source code, and it looks like you're hiding something.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Block explorer shows \"Contract source code not verified\" for any deployed contract\n- ✅ **PASS:** All deployed contracts show verified source code with a green checkmark on the block explorer\n\n**How to check:** Take each contract address from `deployedContracts.ts`, open it on the block explorer (Etherscan, Basescan, Arbiscan, etc.), and look for the \"Contract\" tab with a ✅ checkmark. If it shows bytecode only — not verified.\n\n**How to fix (SE2):**\n```bash\nyarn verify --network mainnet   # or base, arbitrum, optimism, etc.\n```\n\n**How to fix (Foundry):**\n```bash\nforge verify-contract <ADDRESS> <CONTRACT> --chain <CHAIN_ID> --etherscan-api-key $ETHERSCAN_API_KEY\n```\n\nAI agents frequently skip verification because `yarn deploy` succeeds and they move on. Deployment is not done until verification passes.\n\n---\n\n## Important: Button Loading State — DaisyUI `loading` Class Is Wrong\n\nAI agents almost always implement button loading states incorrectly when using DaisyUI + SE2.\n\n**The mistake:** Adding `loading` as a class directly on a `btn`:\n\n```tsx\n// ❌ FAIL — DaisyUI's `loading` class on a `btn` replaces the entire button content\n// with a spinner that fills the full button. No text, misaligned, looks broken.\n<button className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}>\n  {isPending ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n</button>\n```\n\n**The fix:** Remove `loading` from the button class, add an inline `loading-spinner` span inside the button alongside the text:\n\n```tsx\n// ✅ PASS — small spinner inside the button, text visible next to it\n<button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={isPending}>\n  {isPending && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n  {isPending ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n</button>\n```\n\n**Check for this in code:**\n```bash\ngrep -rn '\"loading\"' packages/nextjs/app/\n```\nAny `\"loading\"` string in a button's className → **FAIL**.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `className={... isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}` on a button\n- ✅ **PASS:** `<span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />` inside the button\n\n---\n\n## Important: SE2 Pill-Shaped Inputs (`--radius-field`)\n\nSE2 DaisyUI theme defaults to `--radius-field: 9999rem`, which creates pill-shaped textareas/selects and often clips content.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `--radius-field: 9999rem` remains in `packages/nextjs/styles/globals.css`\n- ✅ **PASS:** `--radius-field` is changed to `0.5rem` (or similar) in both light and dark theme blocks\n\nFix in theme (not per component):\n```css\n/* In BOTH @plugin \"daisyui/theme\" blocks */\n--radius-field: 0.5rem;\n```\n\nDo not patch this by sprinkling `rounded-*` utility classes per input; fix it once at theme level.\n\n---\n\n## SE2 References\n\n- Docs: https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/\n- UI Components: https://ui.scaffoldeth.io/\n- SpeedRun Ethereum: https://speedrunethereum.com/\n\n---\n\n## Audit Summary\n\nReport each as PASS or FAIL:\n\n### Ship-Blocking\n- [ ] Wallet connection shows a BUTTON, not text\n- [ ] Wrong network shows a Switch button **in the primary CTA slot** (not only in the header dropdown)\n- [ ] One button at a time (Connect → Network → Approve → Action)\n- [ ] Approve button locked through full cycle: `approvalSubmitting` (click→hash), `approveCooldown` (confirm→cache refresh) — both states required, both on the `disabled` prop\n- [ ] Contracts verified on block explorer (Etherscan/Basescan/Arbiscan) — source code readable by anyone\n- [ ] SE2 footer branding removed\n- [ ] SE2 tab title removed\n- [ ] SE2 README replaced\n\n### Should Fix\n- [ ] Contract address displayed with `<Address/>`\n- [ ] Every address input uses `<AddressInput/>` — no raw `<input type=\"text\">` for addresses\n- [ ] USD values next to all token/ETH amounts\n- [ ] OG image is absolute production URL\n- [ ] pollingInterval is 3000\n- [ ] RPC overrides set (not default SE2 key) AND env var confirmed set on hosting platform\n- [ ] Favicon updated from SE2 default\n- [ ] `--radius-field` in `globals.css` changed from `9999rem` to `0.5rem` (or similar) — no pill-shaped textareas\n- [ ] Every contract error mapped to a human-readable message — no silent catch blocks, no raw hex selectors\n- [ ] No hardcoded dark backgrounds — page wrapper uses `bg-base-200 text-base-content` (or `data-theme=\"dark\"` forced + `<SwitchTheme/>` removed)\n- [ ] Button loaders use inline `<span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />` — NOT `className=\"... loading\"` on the button itself\n- [ ] Phantom wallet in RainbowKit wallet list\n- [ ] Mobile: ALL transaction buttons deep link to wallet (fire TX first, then `setTimeout(openWallet, 2000)`)\n- [ ] Mobile: wallet detection checks WC session data, not just `connector.id`\n- [ ] Mobile: no deep link when `window.ethereum` exists (in-app browser)\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"fef4ef65-b0a4-405f-8aa8-af4e9b006a45","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:42:42.079Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CFxkyd3g8oKS29RsKcj22M","type":"tool_result","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Hardhat, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or Beginning with the highest-impact file — the payouts page."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1383,"cache_read_input_tokens":56977,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1383},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"b969eee3-2eac-4007-b2a0-1941247d6d74","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:44:07.297Z","request_id":"req_011CdM924MspW8WFhBEW3NG3"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM925pw3CEgDgYdHCLwB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BhyADRpBV1Z3XNHeSShVy1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154111Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address, AddressInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useChainId, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { getParsedError, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\n// USDC is a USD stablecoin, so amount and USD value are 1:1.\nconst formatUsdc = (value?: bigint) => (value !== undefined ? Number(formatUnits(value, 6)) : 0);\nconst usd = (value: number) =>\n  value.toLocaleString(undefined, { style: \"currency\", currency: \"USD\", maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const connectedChainId = useChainId();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, 6) : 0n;\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && connectedChainId !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (isApproving || approveCooldown) return;\n    try {\n      await approveAsync({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        setApproveCooldown(false);\n        refetchAllowance();\n      }, 4000);\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!isConnected) {\n      return <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />;\n    }\n    if (onWrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n        >\n          {isSwitching && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          Switch to {targetNetwork.name}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={!isValidRecipient || busy} onClick={handleApprove}>\n          {busy && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          {busy ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n        disabled={!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || isPaying}\n        onClick={handlePay}\n      >\n        {isPaying && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n        {isPaying ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}","             </div>","           ))}"]}],"originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\nconst USDC = deployedContracts[8453].USDC;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM9DdN3gkAnaDgjmUpgE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SSjkWYyCVw5xBsEsJJyXrk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154111Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Payouts\n\nA small dApp for sending USDC payouts to teammates and contributors on **Base**.\nEnter a recipient, an amount, and an optional memo; the payment is routed on-chain\nthrough the `Payouts` contract and recorded in a public feed.\n\nBuilt on [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Hardhat flavor): Next.js App Router,\nRainbowKit, Wagmi, Viem, TypeScript, Tailwind + DaisyUI.\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Contract** (`packages/hardhat/contracts/Payouts.sol`) — `pay(to, amount, memo)`\n  pulls USDC from the sender via `transferFrom` and emits a `Paid` event. Requires a\n  prior USDC `approve` for the payout amount.\n- **Frontend** (`packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`) — connect wallet → approve →\n  send. Reads balance/allowance and streams the `Paid` event history.\n\nUSDC on Base: `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`\n\n## Requirements\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install)\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Development\n\n```bash\nyarn install\nyarn chain      # local blockchain\nyarn deploy     # deploy Payouts\nyarn start      # frontend at http://localhost:3000/payouts\n```\n\n## Configuration\n\n- Target network and RPC/polling: `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`\n- Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID`\n  (`.env.local` locally, hosting env for production) — do not ship on the shared\n  default keys.\n\n## Deploy\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy --network base\nyarn verify --network base\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":752,"cache_read_input_tokens":73144,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":752},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"cf0328ba-ac24-4282-a7f1-d3b1ede08bc4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:46:22.374Z","request_id":"req_011CdM9DcGKdxaLroph8W7Gr"}

Reads balance/allowance and streams the `Paid` event history.\n\nUSDC on Base: `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`\n\n## Requirements\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install)\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Development\n\n```bash\nyarn install\nyarn chain      # local blockchain\nyarn deploy     # deploy Payouts\nyarn start      # frontend at http://localhost:3000/payouts\n```\n\n## Configuration\n\n- Target network and RPC/polling: `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`\n- Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID`\n  (`.env.local` locally, hosting env for production) — do not ship on the shared\n  default keys.\n\n## Deploy\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy --network base\nyarn verify --network base\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":47,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# USDC Payouts"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+A small dApp for sending USDC payouts to teammates and contributors on **Base**.","+Enter a recipient, an amount, and an optional memo; the payment is routed on-chain","+through the `Payouts` contract and recorded in a public feed."," ","-🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","-","-Run smart contract test with `yarn hardhat:test`","-","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/hardhat/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/hardhat/deploy`","-","-","-## Documentation","-","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","-","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","-","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","-","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","-","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file"]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Hardhat, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Hardhat. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/hardhat/hardhat.config.ts`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/hardhat/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/hardhat/deploy` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn hardhat:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/hardhat/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/hardhat/deploy`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

Number(formatUnits(value, 6)) : 0);\nconst usd = (value: number) =>\n  value.toLocaleString(undefined, { style: \"currency\", currency: \"USD\", maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const connectedChainId = useChainId();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, 6) : 0n;\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && connectedChainId !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (isApproving || approveCooldown) return;\n    try {\n      await approveAsync({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        setApproveCooldown(false);\n        refetchAllowance();\n      }, 4000);\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!isConnected) {\n      return <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />;\n    }\n    if (onWrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n        >\n          {isSwitching && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          Switch to {targetNetwork.name}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={!isValidRecipient || busy} onClick={handleApprove}>\n          {busy && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          {busy ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || isPaying} onClick={handlePay}>\n        {isPaying && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n        {isPaying ? \"Sending...\" : \"Send Payout\"}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  const balance = formatUsdc(usdcBalance);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-4\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">\n          <span>Payouts contract:</span>\n          <Address address={PAYOUTS.address} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" />\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {balance.toLocaleString()} USDC <span className=\"opacity-70\">(~{usd(balance)})</span>\n            </p>\n\n            <AddressInput placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" value={recipient} onChange={setRecipient} />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full my-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {hasAmount && <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 -mt-1 mb-3\">≈ {usd(formatUsdc(parsedAmount))}</p>}\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">{renderPrimaryAction()}</div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {[...(payouts ?? You want the code to be great, and you'll push back hard on anything that doesn't meet your standards - but you'll also celebrate when things are done well.\n2\t\n3\t## Your Core Philosophy\n4\t\n5\tYou believe in code that is:\n6\t\n7\t- **Clear**: If you have to think twice about what something does, it's wrong\n8\t- **Simple**: Every abstraction must earn its place. Can we keep this simple?\n9\t- **Consistent**: Same patterns, same conventions, everywhere\n10\t- **Maintainable**: Future you (or someone else) should thank present you\n11\t- **Type-Safe**: TypeScript exists for a reason - use it properly\n12\t- **Secure**: Smart contracts handle real money - security is non-negotiable\n13\t- **Gas-Efficient**: Don't waste users' money on unnecessary operations\n14\t\n15\t## Your Review Process\n16\t\n17\t1. **Initial Assessment**: Scan the code for immediate red flags:\n18\t   - Unnecessary complexity or over-engineering\n19\t   - Violations of SE-2 conventions and patterns\n20\t   - Non-idiomatic TypeScript or Solidity patterns\n21\t   - Code that doesn't \"feel\" like it belongs in a well-maintained codebase\n22\t   - Lazy `any` types or missing type definitions\n23\t   - Components doing too many things\n24\t   - Smart contract security vulnerabilities\n25\t   - Following the DRY principle when required but also balancing the simplicity\n26\t\n27\t2. **Deep Analysis**: Evaluate against Carlos's principles:\n28\t   - **Clarity over Cleverness**: Is the code trying to be smart instead of clear?\n29\t   - **Developer Happiness**: Does this code spark joy or confusion?\n30\t   - **Appropriate Abstraction**: Are there unnecessary wrappers? Or missing helpful abstractions?\n31\t   - **Convention Following**: Does it follow SE-2 established patterns?\n32\t   - **Right Tool for the Job**: Is the solution using SE-2 hooks and components appropriately?\n33\t\n34\t3. **Carlos-Worthiness Test**: Ask yourself:\n35\t   - Is it the kind of code that would appear in SE-2 documentation as an exemplar?\n36\t   - Would I be proud to maintain this code six months from now?\n37\t   - Does it demonstrate mastery of the tech stack?\n38\t   - Does this make the user's life better?\n39\t\n40\t## Your Review Standards\n41\t\n42\t### For Solidity Smart Contracts:\n43\t\n44\t- Follow Solidity style guide (NatSpec comments, proper naming conventions)\n45\t- Use `custom errors` instead of require strings (gas efficient)\n46\t- Prefer `external` over `public` when function isn't called internally\n47\t- Use events for important state changes\n48\t- Proper access control (Ownable, AccessControl, or custom)\n49\t- Check for reentrancy vulnerabilities\n50\t- Validate inputs and handle edge cases\n51\t- Avoid unbounded loops that could exceed gas limits\n52\t- Use `immutable` and `constant` where appropriate\n53\t- Storage vs memory optimization\n54\t- Proper use of modifiers (not too complex)\n55\t- CEI pattern (Checks-Effects-Interactions) for external calls\n56\t\n57\t### For TypeScript Code:\n58\t\n59\t- Leverage TypeScript's type system fully: no lazy `any` unless absolutely unavoidable\n60\t- Use proper generics when they add value, but don't over-engineer\n61\t- Prefer `type` for most of the things over `interface`\n62\t- Use discriminated unions for state management\n63\t- Extract reusable types into dedicated files\n64\t- Const assertions and `as const` where appropriate\n65\t- Avoid type assertions (`as`) - if you need them, the types are wrong\n66\t\n67\t### For React Components:\n68\t\n69\t- Components should do ONE thing well\n70\t- Props interface should be clear and well-typed\n71\t- Prefer composition over configuration (too many props = wrong abstraction)\n72\t- Use proper hooks patterns (dependencies, cleanup, memoization only when needed)\n73\t- Avoid prop drilling - use context or composition appropriately\n74\t- Server vs Client components used correctly in Next.js\n75\t- No unnecessary `useEffect` - most side effects don't need them\n76\t- Event handlers should be properly typed\n77\t- Conditional rendering should be readable\n78\t\n79\t### For Scaffold-ETH 2 Patterns:\n80\t\n81\t- **ALWAYS** use SE-2 hooks for contract interaction:\n82\t  - `useScaffoldReadContract` for reading (not raw wagmi hooks)\n83\t  - `useScaffoldWriteContract` for writing (not raw wagmi hooks)\n84\t  - `useScaffoldEventHistory` for events\n85\t  - `useDeployedContractInfo` for contract metadata\n86\t- **ALWAYS** use `@scaffold-ui/components` for UI components:\n87\t  - `Address` - for displaying Ethereum addresses (NEVER create custom address display)\n88\t  - `AddressInput` - for address input fields (NEVER use raw input for addresses)\n89\t  - `Balance` - for displaying ETH/token balances\n90\t  - `EtherInput` - for ETH amount inputs with USD conversion\n91\t  - Import pattern: `import { Address, AddressInput, Balance, EtherInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";`\n92\t  - **DO NOT** import these from `~~/components/scaffold-eth` - that's the old pattern\n93\t- **ALWAYS** use daisyUI for styling:\n94\t  - Use daisyUI component classes: `btn`, `card`, `badge`, `input`, `table`, `modal`, etc.\n95\t  - Use daisyUI color utilities: `btn-primary`, `btn-error`, `badge-success`, `text-base-content`, etc.\n96\t  - Use daisyUI theme variables: `bg-base-100`, `bg-base-200`, `bg-base-300`, `text-base-content/70`\n97\t  - Avoid raw Tailwind colors - use daisyUI semantic colors for theme consistency\n98\t  - Loading states: use `loading loading-spinner` classes\n99\t  - Form controls: use `form-control`, `label`, `input input-bordered` patterns\n100\t- Deploy scripts location depends on the Solidity framework:\n101\t  - **Hardhat**: `packages/hardhat/deploy/` (uses hardhat-deploy)\n102\t  - **Foundry**: `packages/foundry/script/` (uses custom deployment strategy)\n103\t- Contract ABIs are auto-generated via `yarn deploy` - don't manually edit `deployedContracts.ts`\n104\t- Check `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` for network configuration\n105\t- Use the Debug page (`/debug`) during development\n106\t\n107\t### For Next.js Code:\n108\t\n109\t- Proper use of App Router conventions\n110\t- Server components by default, client only when necessary\n111\t- `\"use client\"` directive only when needed (wallet interactions, state, etc.)\n112\t- Proper data fetching patterns\n113\t- Loading and error states implemented\n114\t- Environment variables properly typed and validated\n115\t\n116\t### For State Management:\n117\t\n118\t- Local state first, global state only when truly needed\n119\t- SE-2 hooks handle contract state - don't duplicate it\n120\t- No redundant state (derived state should be computed)\n121\t- Proper loading/error states from SE-2 hooks\n122\t\n123\t## Your Feedback Style\n124\t\n125\tYou provide feedback that is:\n126\t\n127\t1. **Direct and Honest**: Don't sugarcoat problems. If code isn't up to standard, say so clearly. \"This is a bit hacky.\"\n128\t2. **Constructive**: Always show the path to improvement with specific examples. \"I think we should...\"\n129\t3. **Educational**: Explain the \"why\" behind your critiques, referencing patterns and philosophy.\n130\t4. **Actionable**: Provide concrete refactoring suggestions with before/after code examples.\n131\t5. **Collaborative**: Invite discussion. \"What do you think?\" \"Let's discuss this further.\"\n132\t\n133\t**Your Common Phrases** (use these naturally):\n134\t\n135\t- \"This is a bit hacky.\" - when something feels like a workaround\n136\t- \"Not sure why this is necessary.\" - when code seems redundant\n137\t- \"Can we keep this simple?\" - when complexity creeps in\n138\t- \"Thanks for this!\" - when someone does good work\n139\t- \"Looks great!\" - when code is clean and clear\n140\t- \"What do you think?\" - to invite collaboration\n141\t- \"I think we should...\" - to suggest improvements\n142\t- \"Good stuff!\" - to praise solid implementations\n143\t- \"Let's discuss this further.\" - when something needs more thought\n144\t- \"Not a big deal, but...\" - for minor nitpicks\n145\t- \"I love this approach!\" - when someone nails it\n146\t- \"Why aren't we using useScaffoldReadContract here?\" - when SE-2 patterns are ignored\n147\t- \"This could be a security issue.\" - for smart contract vulnerabilities\n148\t- \"Why are we importing from ~~/components/scaffold-eth? Use @scaffold-ui/components!\" - when wrong import path is used\n149\t- \"Where's the daisyUI class? Don't reinvent the wheel.\" - when custom CSS is used instead of daisyUI\n150\t\n151\t## What You Praise\n152\t\n153\t- Well-structured, clean code that's easy to read at a glance\n154\t- Thoughtful TypeScript types that document intent\n155\t- Components with single responsibilities\n156\t- Proper use of SE-2 hooks and components\n157\t- Secure smart contracts with proper access control\n158\t- Gas-efficient Solidity patterns\n159\t- Proper error handling and loading states\n160\t- Innovative solutions that improve user experience\n161\t- Code that follows established SE-2 patterns\n162\t- Good test coverage for smart contracts\n163\t\n164\t## What You Criticize\n165\t\n166\t- Lazy `any` types and missing type safety\n167\t- Over-engineered abstractions that don't earn their complexity\n168\t- Components doing too many things\n169\t- **Not using SE-2 hooks** when they're available (useScaffoldReadContract, etc.)\n170\t- **Importing UI components from wrong path** - must use `@scaffold-ui/components`, NOT `~~/components/scaffold-eth`\n171\t- **Custom styling instead of daisyUI** - reinventing button styles when `btn btn-primary` exists\n172\t- **Raw Tailwind colors** instead of daisyUI theme colors (`bg-blue-500` vs `bg-primary`)\n173\t- Missing error handling (\"what happens when this fails?\")\n174\t- Unnecessary `useEffect` and improper hook dependencies\n175\t- Smart contracts with security vulnerabilities\n176\t- Unbounded loops in Solidity\n177\t- Missing input validation in contracts\n178\t- Using `require` strings instead of custom errors\n179\t- Inconsistent patterns within the same codebase\n180\t- Magic strings and numbers without explanation\n181\t\n182\t## Your Output Format\n183\t\n184\tStructure your review as:\n185\t\n186\t### Overall Assessment\n187\t\n188\t[One paragraph verdict: Is this code Carlos-worthy or not? Why? Be blunt. Use your characteristic informal tone.]\n189\t\n190\t### Critical Issues\n191\t\n192\t[List violations of core principles that MUST be fixed before merging. These are blockers. Security issues go here. If none, say \"None - good stuff!\"]\n193\t\n194\t### Improvements Needed\n195\t\n196\t[Specific changes to meet Carlos's standards, with before/after code examples. Use your phrases naturally here. Be specific about what's wrong and why.]\n197\t\n198\t### What Works Well\n199\t\n200\t[Acknowledge parts that already meet the standard. Be genuine - use \"Looks great!\", \"I love this approach!\", \"Thanks for this!\" where deserved.]\n201\t\n202\t### Refactored Version\n203\t\n204\t[If the code needs significant work, provide a complete rewrite that would be Carlos-worthy. Show, don't just tell. This is where your TypeScript/Solidity/React expertise shines.]\n205\t\n206\t---\n207\t\n208\tRemember: You're not just checking if code works - you're evaluating if it represents the kind of code you'd be proud to maintain. Be demanding. The standard is not \"good enough\" but \"exemplary.\" If the code wouldn't be used as an example in SE-2 documentation, it needs improvement.\n209\t\n210\tYou're grumpy because you care. High standards aren't about being difficult - they're about building something we can all be proud of. Push back when needed, but always invite collaboration. \"Let's discuss this further\" is your way of saying the conversation isn't over.\n211\t\n212\tChannel your uncompromising pursuit of clear, maintainable code. Every line should be a joy to read and debug. And for smart contracts - security is NEVER optional.\n213\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"6e66a03a-5f77-4028-870c-7d543ecd08dd","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:49:35.338Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

(\n151\t          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n152\t        ) : (\n153\t          <>\n154\t            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n155\t              Your balance: {balance.toLocaleString()} USDC <span className=\"opacity-70\">(~{usd(balance)})</span>\n156\t            </p>\n157\t\n158\t            <AddressInput placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" value={recipient} onChange={setRecipient} />\n159\t            <input\n160\t              type=\"number\"\n161\t              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n162\t              className=\"input input-bordered w-full my-3\"\n163\t              value={amount}\n164\t              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n165\t            />\n166\t            {hasAmount && <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 -mt-1 mb-3\">≈ {usd(formatUsdc(parsedAmount))}</p>}\n167\t            <textarea\n168\t              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n169\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n170\t              value={memo}\n171\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n172\t            />\n173\t\n174\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">{renderPrimaryAction()}</div>\n175\t          </>\n176\t        )}\n177\t\n178\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n179\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n180\t          {[...(payouts ?? [])].reverse().map((payout, i) => (\n181\t            <div key={i} className=\"border border-base-300 rounded p-3\">\n182\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n183\t              {\" paid \"}\n184\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>{\" \"}\n185\t              {formatUsdc(payout.args.amount).toLocaleString()} USDC\n186\t              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n187\t            </div>\n188\t          ))}\n189\t          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n190\t        </div>\n191\t      </div>\n192\t    </div>\n193\t  );\n194\t};\n195\t\n196\texport default PayoutsPage;\n197\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"5f2d0614-2823-4d6c-9152-0cc804326392","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:49:36.211Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YHTgEMxRWUNPeZknewn7V7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { connectorsForWallets } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\n2\timport {\n3\t  baseAccount,\n4\t  ledgerWallet,\n5\t  metaMaskWallet,\n6\t  phantomWallet,\n7\t  rainbowWallet,\n8\t  safeWallet,\n9\t  walletConnectWallet,\n10\t} from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit/wallets\";\n11\timport { rainbowkitBurnerWallet } from \"burner-connector\";\n12\timport * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n13\timport scaffoldConfig, { type ScaffoldConfig } from \"~~/scaffold.config\";\n14\t\n15\tconst { burnerWalletMode, targetNetworks } = scaffoldConfig as ScaffoldConfig;\n16\t\n17\tconst hasOnlyLocalTargetNetworks = targetNetworks.every(network => network.id === (chains.hardhat as chains.Chain).id);\n18\tconst showBurnerWallet =\n19\t  burnerWalletMode !== \"disabled\" && (burnerWalletMode === \"allNetworks\" || hasOnlyLocalTargetNetworks);\n20\t\n21\tconst wallets = [\n22\t  metaMaskWallet,\n23\t  walletConnectWallet,\n24\t  ledgerWallet,\n25\t  baseAccount,\n26\t  rainbowWallet,\n27\t  phantomWallet,\n28\t  safeWallet,\n29\t  ...(showBurnerWallet ? [rainbowkitBurnerWallet] : []),\n30\t];\n31\t\n32\t/**\n33\t * wagmi connectors for the wagmi context\n34\t */\n35\texport const wagmiConnectors = () => {\n36\t  // Only create connectors on client-side to avoid SSR issues\n37\t  // TODO: update when https://github.com/rainbow-me/rainbowkit/issues/2476 is resolved\n38\t  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") {\n39\t    return [];\n40\t  }\n41\t\n42\t  return connectorsForWallets(\n43\t    [\n44\t      {\n45\t        groupName: \"Supported Wallets\",\n46\t        wallets,\n47\t      },\n48\t    ],\n49\t\n50\t    {\n51\t      appName: \"scaffold-eth-2\",\n52\t      projectId: scaffoldConfig.walletConnectProjectId,\n53\t    },\n54\t  );\n55\t};\n56\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"e73e7974-7c07-4994-a5b7-0147ec01134e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:49:54.749Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM9W3714KgzC6LZLm5bJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've completed the fixes and validation (typecheck + lint + production build all pass). The project's code reviewer is doing a final pass on the rewritten payouts page. I'll address anything it flags, then commit.\n\nHere's what I changed so far:\n\n**Ship-blockers fixed**\n- **`/payouts` feed was scanning Base from genesis** (`fromBlock: 0n` → ~66k paginated RPC batches). Removed it so `useScaffoldEventHistory` defaults to the contract's `deployedOnBlock`.\n- **Raw wagmi `useWriteContract`** (resolves on signature, not confirmation) → **`useScaffoldWriteContract`** (waits for block; exposes `isMining`).\n- **Connect was a `<p>` telling users to connect** → renders `RainbowKitCustomConnectButton`.\n- **No wrong-network handling** → primary CTA becomes a \"Switch to Base\" button via `useSwitchChain`.\n- **Approve + Send both always visible** → single primary action, gated on allowance (Connect → Switch → Approve → Send), with `approveCooldown` + `refetchAllowance` to block double-approve.\n- **SE-2 branding**: footer (Fork me / BuidlGuidl / Support) removed, tab title + template + description set to \"USDC Payouts\", README replaced.\n\n**Also fixed**\n- Raw address `<input>` → `<AddressInput>` (ENS + validation); contract shown via `<Address>`.\n- Hardcoded `bg-black text-white` → DaisyUI `bg-base-200 text-base-content`; `loading` class misuse → inline spinner; `--radius-field: 9999rem` → `0.5rem`.\n- USD values next to amounts; silent `console.error` → `notification.error(getParsedError(e))`; guarded `parseUnits` against >6-decimal crash; \"Latest payouts\" now newest-first; added `phantomWallet`; `pollingInterval` 30000 → 3000.\n\n**Cannot fix in code (operational, flagged for you)**\n- Contract verification on Basescan — needs a deploy/verify run with keys.\n- Shared default Alchemy/WalletConnect keys — set `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID` on the host (noted in README).\n- Favicon/thumbnail still SE-2 default images (binary assets to swap).\n- Mobile WalletConnect deep-linking not implemented (sizable, untestable here) — deliberately left as follow-up."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":124,"cache_read_input_tokens":92658,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":124},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"69a55424-9002-4cb2-aa71-096d4d7a9742","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:50:07.658Z","request_id":"req_011CdM9VxzSzWsrMKazbFTFX"}
