{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FxjDZKHrMPLduAB17eFa1w","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport React from \"react\";\n2\timport Link from \"next/link\";\n3\timport { useFetchNativeCurrencyPrice } from \"@scaffold-ui/hooks\";\n4\timport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\n5\timport { CurrencyDollarIcon, MagnifyingGlassIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\n6\timport { SwitchTheme } from \"~~/components/SwitchTheme\";\n7\timport { Faucet } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\n8\timport { useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth/useTargetNetwork\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * Site footer\n12\t */\n13\texport const Footer = () => {\n14\t  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n15\t  const isLocalNetwork = targetNetwork.id === hardhat.id;\n16\t  const { price: nativeCurrencyPrice } = useFetchNativeCurrencyPrice();\n17\t\n18\t  return (\n19\t    <div className=\"min-h-0 py-5 px-1 mb-11 lg:mb-0\">\n20\t      <div>\n21\t        <div className=\"fixed flex justify-between items-center w-full z-10 p-4 bottom-0 left-0 pointer-events-none\">\n22\t          <div className=\"flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-2 pointer-events-auto\">\n23\t            {nativeCurrencyPrice > 0 && (\n24\t              <div>\n25\t                <div className=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm font-normal gap-1 cursor-auto\">\n26\t                  <CurrencyDollarIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n27\t                  <span>{nativeCurrencyPrice.toFixed(2)}</span>\n28\t                </div>\n29\t              </div>\n30\t            )}\n31\t            {isLocalNetwork && (\n32\t              <>\n33\t                <Faucet />\n34\t                <Link href=\"/blockexplorer\" passHref className=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm font-normal gap-1\">\n35\t                  <MagnifyingGlassIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n36\t                  <span>Block Explorer</span>\n37\t                </Link>\n38\t              </>\n39\t            )}\n40\t          </div>\n41\t          <SwitchTheme className={`pointer-events-auto ${isLocalNetwork ? \"self-end md:self-auto\" : \"\"}`} />\n42\t        </div>\n43\t      </div>\n44\t      <div className=\"w-full\">\n45\t        <ul className=\"menu menu-horizontal w-full\">\n46\t          <div className=\"flex justify-center items-center gap-2 text-sm w-full\">\n47\t            <span className=\"opacity-70\">USDC Payouts on Base</span>\n48\t          </div>\n49\t        </ul>\n50\t      </div>\n51\t    </div>\n52\t  );\n53\t};\n54\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"01e55d45-692e-438b-b0d3-5692c46b5440","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:50:35.177Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CBriKmRh6773CGxQuJ65ZG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\n2\timport type { Metadata } from \"next\";\n3\t\n4\t\n5\tconst baseUrl = process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL\n6\t  ? `https://${process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL}`\n7\t  : `http://localhost:${process.env.PORT || 3000}`;\n8\tconst titleTemplate = \"%s | USDC Payouts\";\n9\t\n10\texport const getMetadata = ({\n11\t  title,\n12\t  description,\n13\t  imageRelativePath = \"/thumbnail.jpg\",\n14\t}: {\n15\t  title: string;\n16\t  description: string;\n17\t  imageRelativePath?: string;\n18\t}): Metadata => {\n19\t  const imageUrl = `${baseUrl}${imageRelativePath}`;\n20\t\n21\t  return {\n22\t  metadataBase: new URL(baseUrl),\n23\t  title: {\n24\t    default: title,\n25\t    template: titleTemplate\n26\t  },\n27\t  description: description,\n28\t  openGraph: {\n29\t    title: {\n30\t      default: title,\n31\t      template: titleTemplate\n32\t    },\n33\t    description: description,\n34\t    images: [\n35\t      {\n36\t        url: imageUrl\n37\t      }\n38\t    ]\n39\t  },\n40\t  twitter: {\n41\t    title: {\n42\t      default: title,\n43\t      template: titleTemplate\n44\t    },\n45\t    description: description,\n46\t    images: [\n47\t      imageUrl\n48\t    ]\n49\t  },\n50\t  icons: {\n51\t    icon: [\n52\t      {\n53\t        url: '/favicon.png',\n54\t        sizes: '32x32',\n55\t        type: 'image/png'\n56\t      }\n57\t    ]\n58\t  }\n59\t};\n60\t}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"6f84fcbb-7447-4a0e-96a1-8fad58f50855","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:50:36.052Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

\"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport {\n11\t  ContractAbi,\n12\t  ContractName,\n13\t  ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n14\t  ScaffoldWriteContractVariables,\n15\t  UseScaffoldWriteConfig,\n16\t  simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError,\n17\t} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n18\t\n19\ttype ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName extends ContractName> = Omit<\n20\t  ReturnType<typeof useWriteContract>,\n21\t  \"writeContract\" | \"writeContractAsync\"\n22\t> & {\n23\t  isMining: boolean;\n24\t  writeContractAsync: <\n25\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n26\t  >(\n27\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n28\t    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n29\t  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n30\t  writeContract: <TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">>(\n31\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n32\t    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n33\t  ) => void;\n34\t};\n35\t\n36\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n37\t  config: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n38\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n39\t/**\n40\t * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n41\t */\n42\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n43\t  contractName: TContractName,\n44\t  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n45\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n46\t\n47\t/**\n48\t * Wrapper around wagmi's useWriteContract hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n49\t * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n50\t * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n51\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n52\t * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n53\t */\n54\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n55\t  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n56\t  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n57\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n58\t  const finalConfig =\n59\t    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n60\t      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n61\t      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n62\t  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n63\t\n64\t  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n65\t\n66\t  useEffect(() => {\n67\t    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n68\t      console.warn(\n69\t        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n70\t      );\n71\t    }\n72\t  }, [configOrName]);\n73\t\n74\t  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n75\t  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n76\t  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n77\t\n78\t  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n79\t\n80\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n81\t\n82\t  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n83\t    contractName,\n84\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n85\t  });\n86\t\n87\t  const sendContractWriteAsyncTx = async <\n88\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n89\t  >(\n90\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n91\t    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n92\t  ) => {\n93\t    if (!deployedContractData) {\n94\t      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n95\t      return;\n96\t    }\n97\t\n98\t    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n99\t      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n100\t      return;\n101\t    }\n102\t\n103\t    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n104\t      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n105\t      return;\n106\t    }\n107\t\n108\t    try {\n109\t      setIsMining(true);\n110\t      const { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation, ...mutateOptions } = options || {};\n111\t\n112\t      const writeContractObject = {\n113\t        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n114\t        address: deployedContractData.address,\n115\t        ...variables,\n116\t      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>;\n117\t\n118\t      if (!finalConfig?.disableSimulate) {\n119\t        await simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError({\n120\t          wagmiConfig,\n121\t          writeContractParams: writeContractObject,\n122\t          chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n123\t        });\n124\t      }\n125\t\n126\t      const makeWriteWithParams = () =>\n127\t        wagmiContractWrite.writeContractAsync(\n128\t          writeContractObject,\n129\t          mutateOptions as\n130\t            | MutateOptions<\n131\t                WriteContractReturnType,\n132\t                WriteContractErrorType,\n133\t                WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n134\t                unknown\n135\t              >\n136\t            | undefined,\n137\t        );\n138\t      const writeTxResult = await writeTx(makeWriteWithParams, { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation });\n139\t\n140\t      return writeTxResult;\n141\t    } catch (e: any) {\n142\t      throw e;\n143\t    } finally {\n144\t      setIsMining(false);\n145\t    }\n146\t  };\n147\t\n148\t  const sendContractWriteTx = <\n149\t    TContractName extends ContractName,\n150\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n151\t  >(\n152\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n153\t    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n154\t  ) => {\n155\t    if (!deployedContractData) {\n156\t      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n157\t      return;\n158\t    }\n159\t    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n160\t      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n161\t      return;\n162\t    }\n163\t\n164\t    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n165\t      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n166\t      return;\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    wagmiContractWrite.writeContract(\n170\t      {\n171\t        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n172\t        address: deployedContractData.address,\n173\t        ...variables,\n174\t      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n175\t      options as\n176\t        | MutateOptions<\n177\t            WriteContractReturnType,\n178\t            WriteContractErrorType,\n179\t            WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n180\t            unknown\n181\t          >\n182\t        | undefined,\n183\t    );\n184\t  };\n185\t\n186\t  return {\n187\t    ...wagmiContractWrite,\n188\t    isMining,\n189\t    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContactAsync\n190\t    writeContractAsync: sendContractWriteAsyncTx,\n191\t    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContract\n192\t    writeContract: sendContractWriteTx,\n193\t  };\n194\t}\n195\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"1c88a614-11cb-4420-8aac-bf2be80eb24b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:50:49.948Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018NGtUTjQdkVMaTvTQrzGJc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\n2\timport { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n3\timport { Abi, AbiEvent, ExtractAbiEventNames } from \"abitype\";\n4\timport { BlockNumber, GetLogsParameters } from \"viem\";\n5\timport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\n6\timport { Config, UsePublicClientReturnType, useBlockNumber, usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";\n7\timport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n8\timport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n9\timport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport { replacer } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/common\";\n11\timport {\n12\t  ContractAbi,\n13\t  ContractName,\n14\t  UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig,\n15\t  UseScaffoldEventHistoryData,\n16\t} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n17\t\n18\tconst getEvents = async (\n19\t  getLogsParams: GetLogsParameters<AbiEvent | undefined, AbiEvent[] | undefined, boolean, BlockNumber, BlockNumber>,\n20\t  publicClient?: UsePublicClientReturnType<Config, number>,\n21\t  Options?: {\n22\t    blockData?: boolean;\n23\t    transactionData?: boolean;\n24\t    receiptData?: boolean;\n25\t  },\n26\t) => {\n27\t  const logs = await publicClient?.getLogs({\n28\t    address: getLogsParams.address,\n29\t    fromBlock: getLogsParams.fromBlock,\n30\t    toBlock: getLogsParams.toBlock,\n31\t    args: getLogsParams.args,\n32\t    event: getLogsParams.event,\n33\t  });\n34\t  if (!logs) return undefined;\n35\t\n36\t  const finalEvents = await Promise.all(\n37\t    logs.map(async log => {\n38\t      return {\n39\t        ...log,\n40\t        blockData:\n41\t          Options?.blockData && log.blockHash ? await publicClient?.getBlock({ blockHash: log.blockHash }) : null,\n42\t        transactionData:\n43\t          Options?.transactionData && log.transactionHash\n44\t            ? await publicClient?.getTransaction({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n45\t            : null,\n46\t        receiptData:\n47\t          Options?.receiptData && log.transactionHash\n48\t            ? await publicClient?.getTransactionReceipt({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n49\t            : null,\n50\t      };\n51\t    }),\n52\t  );\n53\t\n54\t  return finalEvents;\n55\t};\n56\t\n57\t/**\n58\t * @deprecated **Recommended only for local (hardhat/anvil) chains and development.**\n59\t * It uses getLogs which can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s with short block times).\n60\t * For production, use an indexer such as ponder.sh or similar to query contract events efficiently.\n61\t *\n62\t * Reads events from a deployed contract.\n63\t * @param config - The config settings\n64\t * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n65\t * @param config.eventName - name of the event to listen for\n66\t * @param config.fromBlock - optional block number to start reading events from (defaults to `deployedOnBlock` in deployedContracts.ts if set for contract, otherwise defaults to 0)\n67\t * @param config.toBlock - optional block number to stop reading events at (if not provided, reads until current block)\n68\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n69\t * @param config.filters - filters to be applied to the event (parameterName: value)\n70\t * @param config.blockData - if set to true it will return the block data for each event (default: false)\n71\t * @param config.transactionData - if set to true it will return the transaction data for each event (default: false)\n72\t * @param config.receiptData - if set to true it will return the receipt data for each event (default: false)\n73\t * @param config.watch - if set to true, the events will be updated every pollingInterval milliseconds set at scaffoldConfig (default: false)\n74\t * @param config.enabled - set this to false to disable the hook from running (default: true)\n75\t * @param config.blocksBatchSize - optional batch size for fetching events. If specified, each batch will contain at most this many blocks (default: 500)\n76\t */\n77\texport const useScaffoldEventHistory = <\n78\t  TContractName extends ContractName,\n79\t  TEventName extends ExtractAbiEventNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>>,\n80\t  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n81\t  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n82\t  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n83\t>({\n84\t  contractName,\n85\t  eventName,\n86\t  fromBlock,\n87\t  toBlock,\n88\t  chainId,\n89\t  filters,\n90\t  blockData,\n91\t  transactionData,\n92\t  receiptData,\n93\t  watch,\n94\t  enabled = true,\n95\t  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n96\t}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n97\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n98\t\n99\t  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n100\t  useEffect(() => {\n101\t    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n102\t      console.log(\n103\t        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. It can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s)\",\n104\t      );\n105\t    }\n106\t  }, [selectedNetwork.id]);\n107\t\n108\t  const publicClient = usePublicClient({\n109\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n110\t  });\n111\t  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);\n112\t  const [lastFetchedBlock, setLastFetchedBlock] = useState<bigint | null>(null);\n113\t  const [isPollingActive, setIsPollingActive] = useState(false);\n114\t\n115\t  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({ watch: watch, chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n116\t\n117\t  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n118\t    contractName,\n119\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n120\t  });"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"23ebbeb2-c6de-461d-981e-ab7857a3fb37","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:51:06.899Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM9bs4vVxyMe942yvAXc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've reviewed everything. Here's the verdict.\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\nDecent bones, but not ship-ready. The migration to `useScaffoldWriteContract`, dropping `fromBlock:0n` (correctly falls back to `deployedOnBlock` 33712450 per the hook), `AddressInput`, and pollingInterval 3000 for an L2 are all the right calls. But the allowance gating — the whole point of the rewrite — has a real hole, the approve flow has an unnecessary hacky timer, and you reinvented address display in the payouts list. Fix those before shipping money-moving code.\n\n## Critical / correctness (blockers)\n\n**1. `needsApproval` treats \"allowance still loading\" as \"no approval needed\" — page.tsx:75**\n```ts\nconst needsApproval = hasAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount;\n```\nWhile `allowance === undefined` (initial load, or right after connect before the read resolves), `needsApproval` is `false`, so `renderPrimaryAction` falls straight through to the enabled **Send Payout** button. User clicks, `pay` calls `transferFrom` with zero allowance → simulate/tx revert. This is exactly the \"gated on allowance\" flow you set out to build, and it's not gated during the loading window. Treat `allowance === undefined` as not-ready (disable + spinner), e.g. gate the Send branch on `allowance !== undefined`.\n\n**2. `approveCooldown` + 4s `setTimeout` is a hacky workaround, not needed — page.tsx:82-86**\n`writeContractAsync` already awaits block confirmation via `useTransactor` (see useScaffoldWriteContract.ts:138), and scaffold reads auto-refetch on new blocks. So by the time `await approveAsync(...)` returns, the approval is mined. You then force an extra 4s of \"Approving...\" on an already-done tx, and the timer isn't cleared on unmount (state-update-after-unmount warning). Drop the cooldown entirely; rely on `isApproving` for double-click protection and a single `refetchAllowance()` right after the await. Can we keep this simple?\n\n## SE-2 convention violations\n\n**3. Custom `shorten()` address display instead of `<Address>` — page.tsx:20, 182-184**\nYou import `Address` but then hand-roll `shorten(payout.args.from)` in the payouts list. The guidance is explicit: never build custom address display. Use `<Address address={payout.args.to} size=\"sm\" />` — you get ENS, blockie, and explorer links for free. This is the \"don't reinvent the wheel\" one.\n\n**4. Hardcoded `deployedContracts[8453].Payouts` + chain id — page.tsx:18, 63, 81, 94**\nReaching into the generated map with a magic `8453` to get the spender address is brittle and off-pattern. Use `useScaffoldContract({ contractName: \"Payouts\" })` (or `useDeployedContractInfo`) so the address resolves through the same network-aware path as your write hooks. If someone adds a second target network, this silently breaks.\n\n## Improvements (non-blocking)\n\n- **Dead code — page.tsx:104-107**: `renderPrimaryAction`'s `if (!isConnected)` branch is unreachable; the function is only called inside the `isConnected` branch of the JSX (page.tsx:150-176). The connect button is already rendered by the outer ternary. Remove the dead branch.\n- **`safeParseUsdc` silently zeroes >6-decimal input — page.tsx:23-29**: no crash, good, but the button just goes quietly disabled with zero feedback. At least surface \"USDC has 6 decimals max.\" Not a big deal, but it's a silent failure.\n- **No balance check**: nothing stops `amount > usdcBalance`; the tx just reverts. A cheap pre-check would save gas and confusion.\n- **`key={i}` on the reversed events list — page.tsx:180-181**: index keys on a `watch:true` list that prepends. Use `${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}`.\n- **`useScaffoldEventHistory` on Base is deprecated for production** (see the hook's own `@deprecated` note, useScaffoldEventHistory.ts:57-60): `getLogs` from block ~33.7M with `watch` polling every 3s will hammer the RPC. For a pre-ship Base app, consider the `ponder` or `subgraph` skill for the payouts feed. Fine for a demo, risky for prod.\n\n## What works well\n\n- Migration to `useScaffoldWriteContract` with `isMining` for confirmation gating — good stuff.\n- Dropping `fromBlock:0n` so it defaults to `deployedOnBlock` — correct, no more genesis scan on Base. I love this.\n- `AddressInput` for recipient, `Address` for the contract header, DaisyUI theme classes (`bg-base-200`, `text-base-content`, `loading loading-spinner`, `btn btn-primary`) throughout — clean and on-pattern.\n- `getParsedError` + `notification.error` in both handlers — proper error surfacing.\n- pollingInterval 3000, `phantomWallet` addition, Footer/metadata rebrand — all fine and consistent. `getMetadata.ts` formatting is messy (prettier will complain) but functionally correct.\n\nFiles reviewed: `packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx` (the one with the real problems), `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, `packages/nextjs/services/web3/wagmiConnectors.tsx`, `packages/nextjs/components/Footer.tsx`, `packages/nextjs/app/layout.tsx`, `packages/nextjs/utils/scaffold-eth/getMetadata.ts`, `packages/nextjs/styles/globals.css`.\n\nNet: items 1-4 before merge. Let's discuss further if you disagree on the allowance-loading gate — but I'm pretty firm on that one, it's real money."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3250,"cache_read_input_tokens":31299,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3250,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_013QRskqJZpaK15FP26a2Rj6","session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"6c96aa20-8133-496b-91e2-b907d8590c8e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:52:18.655Z","request_id":"req_011CdM9br1BFCk4GavHyqgji","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const connectedChainId = useChainId();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = safeParseUsdc(amount);\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && connectedChainId !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (isApproving || approveCooldown) return;\n    try {\n      await approveAsync({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        setApproveCooldown(false);\n        refetchAllowance();\n      }, 4000);\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!isConnected) {\n      return <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />;\n    }\n    if (onWrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n        >\n          {isSwitching && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          Switch to {targetNetwork.name}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={!isValidRecipient || busy} onClick={handleApprove}>\n          {busy && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          {busy ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={!isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || isPaying} onClick={handlePay}>\n        {isPaying && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n        {isPaying ? \"Sending...\" : \"Send Payout\"}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  const balance = formatUsdc(usdcBalance);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-4\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        <div </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM9mYahuCsBTioenSJC5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EMkqsWRSgxmDf2LM6gVavd","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154111Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address, AddressInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useChainId, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { getParsedError, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\n// parseUnits throws on >6 decimals or malformed input; treat those as no amount.\nconst safeParseUsdc = (value: string) => {\n  try {\n    return value ? parseUnits(value, 6) : 0n;\n  } catch {\n    return 0n;\n  }\n};\n\n// USDC is a USD stablecoin, so amount and USD value are 1:1.\nconst formatUsdc = (value?: bigint) => (value !== undefined ? Number(formatUnits(value, 6)) : 0);\nconst usd = (value: number) =>\n  value.toLocaleString(undefined, { style: \"currency\", currency: \"USD\", maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const connectedChainId = useChainId();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: payoutsInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Payouts\" });\n  const payoutsAddress = payoutsInfo?.address;\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, payoutsAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const parsedAmount = safeParseUsdc(amount);\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const tooManyDecimals = amount !== \"\" && !hasAmount && Number(amount) > 0;\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const insufficientBalance = hasAmount && usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;\n  const allowanceReady = allowance !== undefined;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && allowanceReady && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const onWrongNetwork = isConnected && connectedChainId !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (isApproving || !payoutsAddress) return;\n    try {\n      await approveAsync({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [payoutsAddress, parsedAmount] });\n      await refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (isPaying) return;\n    try {\n      await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      await refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (onWrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n        >\n          {isSwitching && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          Switch to {targetNetwork.name}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    const inputsInvalid = !isValidRecipient || !hasAmount || insufficientBalance;\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={inputsInvalid || isApproving} onClick={handleApprove}>\n          {isApproving && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n          {isApproving ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    // Until the allowance read resolves we can't know whether an approval is needed, so block Send.\n    return (\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n        disabled={inputsInvalid || !allowanceReady || isPaying}\n        onClick={handlePay}\n      >\n        {isPaying && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n        {isPaying ? \"Sending...\" : \"Send Payout\"}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  const balance = formatUsdc(usdcBalance);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-4\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">\n          <span>Payouts contract:</span>\n          {payoutsAddress && <Address address={payoutsAddress} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" />}\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {balance.toLocaleString()} USDC <span className=\"opacity-70\">(~{usd(balance)})</span>\n            </p>\n\n            <AddressInput placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" value={recipient} onChange={setRecipient} />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full my-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {hasAmount && <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 -mt-1 mb-3\">≈ {usd(formatUsdc(parsedAmount))}</p>}\n            {tooManyDecimals && <p className=\"text-sm text-error -mt-1 mb-3\">USDC supports up to 6 decimals.</p>}\n            {insufficientBalance && <p className=\"text-sm text-error -mt-1 mb-3\">Amount exceeds your balance.</p>}\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">{renderPrimaryAction()}</div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {[...(payouts ??   notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n      setRecipient(\"\");\n      refetchAllowance();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notification.error(getParsedError(e));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">\n          <span>Payouts contract:</span>\n          <Address address={PAYOUTS.address} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" />\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {balance.toLocaleString()} USDC <span className=\"opacity-70\">(~{usd(balance)})</span>\n            </p>\n\n            <AddressInput placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" value={recipient} onChange={setRecipient} />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full my-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {hasAmount && <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 -mt-1 mb-3\">≈ {usd(formatUsdc(parsedAmount))}</p>}\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">{renderPrimaryAction()}</div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {[...(payouts ?? [])].reverse().map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-base-300 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>{\" \"}\n              {formatUsdc(payout.args.amount).toLocaleString()} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","userModified":false}}

The final pass is committed on branch `ship-ready-payouts` (commit `d324fe5`), with typecheck, lint, and a production build all green, and the pre-commit hook passing.\n\n## What I fixed\n\n**Ship-blockers (payouts page)**\n- **Feed scanned Base from genesis** — `fromBlock: 0n` forced ~66k paginated RPC batches; removed so it defaults to the contract's `deployedOnBlock`.\n- **Raw wagmi `useWriteContract`** (resolves on signature) → **`useScaffoldWriteContract`** (waits for block confirmation).\n- **Connect was a `<p>`** → `RainbowKitCustomConnectButton`.\n- **No wrong-network guard** → primary CTA becomes a \"Switch to Base\" button.\n- **Approve + Send both always shown** → one CTA at a time, gated on allowance (Connect → Switch → Approve → Send).\n- **SE-2 branding** removed from footer, tab title, and README.\n\n**From the code review (all four addressed)**\n- Send button was enabled while the allowance read was still loading → could fire `pay` with zero allowance and revert. Now Send is blocked until the allowance resolves.\n- Dropped the hacky 4s `approveCooldown` timer (leaked on unmount); rely on `isMining` + `await refetchAllowance()`.\n- Feed rows now use `<Address>` (ENS/blockie/explorer) instead of a hand-rolled `shorten()`.\n- Resolve the contract via `useDeployedContractInfo` instead of the magic `deployedContracts[8453]` lookup.\n- Plus: balance check, >6-decimal input guard with a message, stable list keys, dead-branch removal.\n\n**Config/style**: `pollingInterval` 30000 → 3000, added `phantomWallet`, `--radius-field` 9999rem → 0.5rem, DaisyUI theme colors, inline spinner, `notification.error(getParsedError(e))`.\n\n## Can't be fixed in code — needs you before launch (also saved to memory)\n- **Verify the Payouts contract on Basescan** (`yarn verify --network base`).\n- **Set real `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID`** on the host — repo still ships shared SE-2 defaults.\n- **Swap the SE-2 default favicon/thumbnail** images.\n- **Mobile WalletConnect deep-linking** left as a follow-up (sizable, untestable here).\n\nI did not touch `Payouts.sol` — it's already deployed, so source edits wouldn't change the on-chain bytecode, and its `transferFrom` bool-check is correct for USDC. The branch isn't pushed; say the word if you want it pushed or a PR opened."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":339,"cache_read_input_tokens":115791,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":339},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9bc549d-9766-4109-89c3-049d2d6bc089","uuid":"19c72036-a612-41e5-b1f5-fa2032c2fbc8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:56:30.794Z","request_id":"req_011CdM9zTef6zqpLiTjhYsyn"}
